# Unverified Leads and Complete Original-Claim Disposition

Everything in this file is a research lead or an accounting record, not an asserted vessel, harbor or operational fact. Do not import its old numbers into parameters or tests. A prior audit verdict is recorded as historical review evidence only; a retained claim requires the fresh read documented in the verification log.

## How to read the accounting

The accounting unit is a nonempty original prose/equation/list line or table data row, including scope assertions and source descriptions. A unit can contain several factual clauses; the disposition covers **every clause and field**, not just the topic label. Headings, blank lines, separators and table-column headings are formatting and excluded. Pure questions/recommendations are recorded too so embedded assumptions cannot disappear silently.

For a narrowed unit, **only** the explicitly linked new claim IDs survive; every other original assertion, qualification, number, explanation and inference in that unit is moved here. A reference to a new claim does not retain the whole old row. For a moved unit, the entire old assertion is withheld. “Unsupported” includes not freshly verified in this rewrite and does not mean false. Original file:line and hashes below identify the exact immutable input. Topic labels are navigation summaries, not restatements endorsed as facts.

## High-impact leads

| Lead | Removed assertion or estimate to investigate | Reason | Evidence needed to settle it |
|---|---|---|---|
| H01 | T lightship 48–62 t/full load 65–82 t; Rockaway 56–68 t/75–92 t; K 72–90 t/105–128 t; inferred engine and repower weight increments | Extrapolated | Vessel-specific hydrostatics, weight report and load case; otherwise reproducible mass budget with explicitly estimated inputs. |
| H02 | Turning 4.2–6.5 L, crash stop 22–35 s or 90–165 m, acceleration 35–55 s; all alternate harbor/Rockaway bands | Extrapolated; trial literature not reverified | Actual vessel trials with controls, load, depth and environmental conditions; no comparator-to-NYC transfer without a derivation. |
| H03 | Idle 600–750 rpm, clutch/reversal delays, ±30–35° rudders, 5–8 s steering slew | Unsupported/extrapolated | Identified installed control and gear manuals plus timing observations. |
| H04 | Shaft spacing 5.6–6.2 m, inferred demihull/wetdeck dimensions, propeller pitch/handedness and K diameter | Extrapolated | As-built dimensioned hull, shaft, rudder and propeller plans. |
| H05 | Split-engine thrust, 4.0–9.5°/s pivot rates, yaw acceleration and claimed monohull advantage | Extrapolated | Vessel-specific force/geometry and inertia/damping evidence or observed pivots with RPM/load/current. |
| H06 | Windage areas/coefficients, added mass, maneuvering derivatives, resistance multipliers and squat bands | Extrapolated; some cited papers unavailable in the audit | Readable equation/test sources with normalization and reproducible NYC geometry/input mapping. |
| H07 | K-Class Jastram/Humphree installation; interceptor trim/squat/turn effects and modes | Unsupported by the read delivery announcement | Hull-specific equipment record and performance evidence; T09 supports only the described River installation. |
| H08 | Per-demihull fuel explanation, crew/lifesaving explanation, metric-hp author intent, draft typo, loading-speed and SCR tank-space explanations | Unsupported | A third public primary source explicitly explaining each discrepancy; retain the disagreement until then. |
| H09 | Complete fleet totals, current active status, MMSIs/call signs, most ON/date/tonnage/registered dimensions; H93 cancellation | Unsupported beyond narrow designer/historical records | Public authoritative register joined to hull-specific builder records; complete status date and population definition. |
| H10 | Per-landing standard dimensions and berth counts, push-knee pockets/ramp placement, contact friction/stiffness, holding thrust and dwell times | Unsupported/extrapolated | Landing-specific public as-built survey and permitted observation; stop points and generic impact design do not settle these. |
| H11 | Route shape distances, cumulative trip times and current route membership at every stop | Unsupported as retained calculations | Versioned feed, selected trip/service date, exact geometry and reproducible distance/time calculation. |
| H12 | All original monthly mean/max current speeds, phase delays, numerical hazard currents and generalized spatial current fields | Unsupported by station metadata | Product/station/bin/date/timezone/units/interval response plus aggregation and validation method. |
| H13 | GT as spring range, percentage spring-tide increase, generalized harbor datum/chart claims | Contradicted interpretation or unsupported extrapolation | Correct datum definitions, station arithmetic and dataset-specific vertical reference; D01 is explicitly derived. |
| H14 | Local no-wake cutoffs, weather limits, named eddy mechanisms, universal passing customs and operator radio/manning procedures | Unsupported beyond the narrow retained legal text | Exact current public rule, Coast Pilot passage or operator-approved public procedure with scope. |
| H15 | Wrong NTSB identifiers and casualty-derived NYC numerical handling claims; Flyer/Zelinsky details and vessel explanations | Contradicted identifiers, unavailable bulletin or unsupported inference | Actual primary incident report with matched vessel/date; no invented substitute identifier. |
| H16 | Completed repowers, actual February 16 restoration and already-effective November 2 relocation | Unsupported completion/status inference | Public completion or in-effect record; K10–K11, L02 and L03 are announcements with their own temporal limits. |

## Per-file evidence requests

- **vessel-t-class.md** — Geometry, capacities, machinery, construction history and performance. Required evidence: Vessel-specific public drawing, equipment/rating sheet, trial or registration record, with configuration and units.
- **vessel-t-class-rockaway.md** — Rockaway identity, specifications, incident history and inferred handling. Required evidence: Hull-specific public identity/configuration records and trials; do not infer a variant from name or engine family.
- **vessel-k-class.md** — K-Class identity, capacities, machinery and proposed repower effects. Required evidence: Configuration-dated builder/drawing or public completion evidence; exact equipment/rating and loading conditions.
- **fleet-roster.md** — Fleet counts, names, identities, dates, tonnage and registered dimensions. Required evidence: Authoritative public registration and builder delivery record for every field, including hull/name/MMSI joins.
- **routes-and-landings.md** — Route schedules/distances, landing infrastructure and operating procedures. Required evidence: Versioned feed with exact trip/date/query or surveyed landing drawing; public operator notice for operational assertions.
- **harbor-environment.md** — Current statistics, datum interpretation, hazards, weather and wake rules. Required evidence: Complete NOAA product request and response plus reproducible calculation, or exact Coast Pilot paragraph and edition.
- **regulations-and-procedures.md** — Regulatory interpretations, VTS procedures, manning and casualty details. Required evidence: Exact current legal section or actual casualty document, with paragraph/page and vessel-specific applicability.
- **comparable-vessel-handling.md** — Comparator fields and all proposed NYC physics bands. Required evidence: Readable primary vessel/trial source; for transferred estimates, a reproducible formula, all inputs, uncertainties and applicability.
- **conflicts.md** — Claimed reconciliations and explanations. Required evidence: Both original source statements plus a separately read primary source that explicitly resolves the disagreement.
- **crew-questions.md** — Factual premises and promises embedded in crew questions. Required evidence: Public vessel-specific evidence or operator-approved non-sensitive observation; remove assumptions embedded in questions.
- **README.md** — Overview claims, suggested bounds and promises of verification. Required evidence: The exact vessel-specific public records or reproducible observation/derivation requested in the revised unknowns list.
- **sources.md** — Bibliographic and scope/relevance assertions. Required evidence: Fetch and read the named document; verify identity, bibliographic details and the exact asserted relevance.
